```python
             * (XAxisKind, RollMethod) allowed-pair table
             * tenor-token grammar matches parent's x_axis_kind
             * discount-curve default & basis invariants
             * every active currency has at least one discount curve
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from riskquant.config.enums.asset_classes import (
    ASSET_SUBCLASS_TO_CLASS,
    VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
    VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
    AssetClass,
    AssetSubclass,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.discount_basis import (
    DISCOUNT_BASES_EXCLUDING_SENTINEL,
    FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS,
    VALID_DISCOUNT_BASES,
    DiscountBasis,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.roll_methods import (
    ALLOWED_X_AXIS_KIND_ROLL_METHOD_PAIRS,
    VALID_ROLL_METHODS,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.unit_types import VALID_UNIT_SCALES, VALID_UNIT_TYPES
from riskquant.config.enums.x_axis_kinds import (
    VALID_X_AXIS_KINDS,
    X_AXIS_KINDS_REQUIRING_ROLL,
    X_AXIS_TOKEN_REGEX,
    XAxisKind,
)
from riskquant.config.enums.y_axis_kinds import VALID_Y_AXIS_KINDS
from riskquant.config.namespace import GlobalIdNamespace, enforce_id_namespace
from riskquant.exceptions import ConfigurationError
from riskquant.market_data.curves.extrap_methods import EXTRAP_METHOD_NAMES
from riskquant.market_data.curves.interp_methods import INTERP_METHOD_NAMES
from riskquant.market_data.risk_factors.shift_types import SHIFT_TYPE_NAMES

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

REQUIRED_SHEETS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "ccy",
        "holiday_calendars",
        "spot_roll_rule",
        "node_meta",
        "curve_meta",
        "curve_point_meta",
        "surface_meta",
        "surface_point_meta",
    }
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Generic helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _check_required_sheets(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    missing = REQUIRED_SHEETS - dict_of_dfs.keys()
    if missing:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Missing required sheet(s): {sorted(missing)}.\n"
            f"Required: {sorted(REQUIRED_SHEETS)}"
        )


def _check_columns(df: pd.DataFrame, cols: frozenset[str], sheet_name: str) -> None:
    missing = cols - set(df.columns)
    if missing:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Sheet {sheet_name!r} missing required columns: {sorted(missing)}.\n"
            f"Found: {sorted(df.columns)}"
        )


def _check_unique(df: pd.DataFrame, col: str, sheet_name: str) -> None:
    if not df[col].is_unique:
        dupes = df[col][df[col].duplicated()].unique().tolist()
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Uniqueness violation: {sheet_name}.{col} must be unique. "
            f"Duplicates: {dupes}"
        )


def _check_composite_unique(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    cols: tuple[str, ...],
    sheet_name: str,
) -> None:
    dupes_mask = df.duplicated(subset=list(cols), keep=False)
    if dupes_mask.any():
        offending = df.loc[dupes_mask, list(cols)]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Composite uniqueness violation: "
            f"{sheet_name}({', '.join(cols)}) must be unique.\n"
            f"Duplicates:\n{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _check_enum_col(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    col: str,
    valid_set: frozenset[str],
    sheet_name: str,
    enum_name: str,
    *,
    nullable: bool,
) -> None:
    if col not in df.columns:
        return
    series = df[col]
    populated = series.dropna()
    if not nullable and len(populated) != len(series):
        n_null = int(series.isna().sum())
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Enum column {sheet_name}.{col} ({enum_name}) "
            f"contains {n_null} null value(s) but is non-nullable."
        )
    if populated.empty:
        return
    invalid = set(populated.astype(str)) - valid_set
    if invalid:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Enum violation in {sheet_name}.{col}: values not in {enum_name}: "
            f"{sorted(invalid)}.\nValid: {sorted(valid_set)}"
        )


def _check_fk(
    child_df: pd.DataFrame,
    child_col: str,
    parent_df: pd.DataFrame,
    parent_col: str,
    *,
    child_sheet: str,
    parent_sheet: str,
) -> None:
    child_vals = child_df[child_col].dropna().to_numpy()
    parent_vals = parent_df[parent_col].dropna().to_numpy()
    missing = np.setdiff1d(child_vals, parent_vals, assume_unique=False)
    if missing.size > 0:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"FK violation: {child_sheet}.{child_col} references values absent "
            f"from {parent_sheet}.{parent_col}.\nMissing: {missing.tolist()[:25]}"
        )


def _check_no_blank_strings(
    df: pd.DataFrame, col: str, sheet_name: str, *, nullable: bool
) -> None:
    if col not in df.columns:
        return
    series = df[col]
    if nullable:
        series = series.dropna()
    if series.empty:
        return
    is_blank = series.astype(str).str.strip() == ""
    if is_blank.any():
        idx = series.index[is_blank].tolist()[:10]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Blank string violation: {sheet_name}.{col} has empty values at "
            f"rows: {idx}"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 2 — per-sheet uniqueness
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    single_pk: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
        "ccy": ("ccy_name", "ccy_id"),
        "spot_roll_rule": ("rule_name",),
        "node_meta": ("node_id", "node_name"),
        "curve_meta": ("curve_id", "curve_name"),
        "curve_point_meta": ("rf_id",),
        "surface_meta": ("surface_id", "surface_name"),
        "surface_point_meta": ("rf_id",),
    }
    for sheet_name, cols in single_pk.items():
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet_name]
        for col in cols:
            _check_unique(df, col, sheet_name)

    _check_composite_unique(
        dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"],
        ("curve_id", "tenor_token"),
        "curve_point_meta",
    )
    _check_composite_unique(
        dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"],
        ("surface_id", "x_tenor_token", "y_label"),
        "surface_point_meta",
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 3 — enum membership
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_enums(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    interp_names = frozenset(INTERP_METHOD_NAMES)
    extrap_names = frozenset(EXTRAP_METHOD_NAMES)
    shift_names = frozenset(SHIFT_TYPE_NAMES)

    # --- node_meta ---
    node_df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "asset_class",
        VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
        "node_meta",
        "AssetClass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "asset_subclass",
        VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
        "node_meta",
        "AssetSubclass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df, "unit_type", VALID_UNIT_TYPES, "node_meta", "UnitType", nullable=False
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df,
        "unit_scale",
        VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
        "node_meta",
        "UnitScale",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        node_df, "shift_type", shift_names, "node_meta", "ShiftType", nullable=False
    )

    # --- curve_meta ---
    curve_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "asset_class",
        VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
        "curve_meta",
        "AssetClass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "asset_subclass",
        VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
        "curve_meta",
        "AssetSubclass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "unit_type",
        VALID_UNIT_TYPES,
        "curve_meta",
        "UnitType",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "unit_scale",
        VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
        "curve_meta",
        "UnitScale",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df, "shift_type", shift_names, "curve_meta", "ShiftType", nullable=False
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "x_axis_kind",
        VALID_X_AXIS_KINDS,
        "curve_meta",
        "XAxisKind",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "x_interp_method",
        interp_names,
        "curve_meta",
        "InterpMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "x_extrap_method",
        extrap_names,
        "curve_meta",
        "ExtrapMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        curve_df,
        "discount_basis",
        VALID_DISCOUNT_BASES,
        "curve_meta",
        "DiscountBasis",
        nullable=False,
    )

    # --- surface_meta ---
    surf_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "asset_class",
        VALID_ASSET_CLASSES,
        "surface_meta",
        "AssetClass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "asset_subclass",
        VALID_ASSET_SUBCLASSES,
        "surface_meta",
        "AssetSubclass",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "unit_type",
        VALID_UNIT_TYPES,
        "surface_meta",
        "UnitType",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "unit_scale",
        VALID_UNIT_SCALES,
        "surface_meta",
        "UnitScale",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df, "shift_type", shift_names, "surface_meta", "ShiftType", nullable=False
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "x_axis_kind",
        VALID_X_AXIS_KINDS,
        "surface_meta",
        "XAxisKind",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "x_interp_method",
        interp_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "InterpMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "x_extrap_method",
        extrap_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "ExtrapMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "y_axis_kind",
        VALID_Y_AXIS_KINDS,
        "surface_meta",
        "YAxisKind",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "y_interp_method",
        interp_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "InterpMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )
    _check_enum_col(
        surf_df,
        "y_extrap_method",
        extrap_names,
        "surface_meta",
        "ExtrapMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )

    # --- spot_roll_rule ---
    srr_df = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]
    _check_enum_col(
        srr_df,
        "roll_method",
        VALID_ROLL_METHODS,
        "spot_roll_rule",
        "RollMethod",
        nullable=False,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 4 — foreign keys
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_fks(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    ccy_df = dict_of_dfs["ccy"]
    node_df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    curve_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    curve_pt_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]
    surf_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    surf_pt_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]
    srr_df = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]

    # ccy FKs
    _check_fk(
        node_df,
        "quote_ccy",
        ccy_df,
        "ccy_name",
        child_sheet="node_meta",
        parent_sheet="ccy",
    )
    _check_fk(
        curve_df,
        "quote_ccy",
        ccy_df,
        "ccy_name",
        child_sheet="curve_meta",
        parent_sheet="ccy",
    )
    _check_fk(
        surf_df,
        "quote_ccy",
        ccy_df,
        "ccy_name",
        child_sheet="surface_meta",
        parent_sheet="ccy",
    )

    # node_meta.fx_base_ccy is nullable; FK only on populated rows
    populated_fx_base = node_df.loc[node_df["fx_base_ccy"].notna(), ["fx_base_ccy"]]
    if not populated_fx_base.empty:
        _check_fk(
            populated_fx_base,
            "fx_base_ccy",
            ccy_df,
            "ccy_name",
            child_sheet="node_meta",
            parent_sheet="ccy",
        )

    # Curve / surface spot_roll_rule_name FKs (only populated rows)
    cm_pop = curve_df.loc[
        curve_df["spot_roll_rule_name"].notna(), ["spot_roll_rule_name"]
    ]
    if not cm_pop.empty:
        _check_fk(
            cm_pop,
            "spot_roll_rule_name",
            srr_df,
            "rule_name",
            child_sheet="curve_meta",
            parent_sheet="spot_roll_rule",
        )

    sm_pop = surf_df.loc[
        surf_df["spot_roll_rule_name"].notna(), ["spot_roll_rule_name"]
    ]
    if not sm_pop.empty:
        _check_fk(
            sm_pop,
            "spot_roll_rule_name",
            srr_df,
            "rule_name",
            child_sheet="surface_meta",
            parent_sheet="spot_roll_rule",
        )

    # Curve / surface point parent FKs
    _check_fk(
        curve_pt_df,
        "curve_id",
        curve_df,
        "curve_id",
        child_sheet="curve_point_meta",
        parent_sheet="curve_meta",
    )
    _check_fk(
        surf_pt_df,
        "surface_id",
        surf_df,
        "surface_id",
        child_sheet="surface_point_meta",
        parent_sheet="surface_meta",
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tier 5 — bespoke domain invariants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _validate_global_name_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """No name appears in more than one of {node, curve, surface} namespaces."""
    node_names = set(dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]["node_name"].astype(str))
    curve_names = set(dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]["curve_name"].astype(str))
    surface_names = set(dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]["surface_name"].astype(str))

    nc = node_names & curve_names
    ns = node_names & surface_names
    cs = curve_names & surface_names

    overlap = nc | ns | cs
    if overlap:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Name collision across container namespaces: {sorted(overlap)}.\n"
            f"Every node_name / curve_name / surface_name must be globally unique."
        )


def _validate_rf_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """rf_ids (node_id ∪ curve_point.rf_id ∪ surface_point.rf_id) must be globally unique."""
    node_ids = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]["node_id"].to_numpy()
    cp_ids = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()
    sp_ids = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()

    all_rf_ids = np.concatenate([node_ids, cp_ids, sp_ids])

    if np.unique(all_rf_ids).size != all_rf_ids.size:
        unique, counts = np.unique(all_rf_ids, return_counts=True)
        dupes = unique[counts > 1].tolist()
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"CRITICAL: rf_id collision across atomic namespaces: {dupes[:25]}.\n"
            f"node_id, curve_point.rf_id, and surface_point.rf_id share one ID space "
            f"and cannot overlap."
        )


def _validate_container_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """Ensure internal primary key uniqueness within container tables."""
    # Cross-container collisions are prevented by the namespace bounds.
    # We only need to check that the tables themselves don't have duplicate IDs.
    for table, col in [("curve_meta", "curve_id"), ("surface_meta", "surface_id")]:
        ids = dict_of_dfs[table][col].to_numpy()
        if np.unique(ids).size != ids.size:
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"CRITICAL: Duplicate primary keys found in {table}.{col}."
            )


def _validate_global_id_namespace(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """IDs that share a namespace must be globally bounded."""
    # 1. Gather ALL atomic Risk Factor IDs (Dimension = 0)
    node_ids = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"][
        "node_id"
    ].to_numpy()  # Node IDs are RF IDs by definition
    cp_ids = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()
    sp_ids = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]["rf_id"].to_numpy()
    all_rf_ids = np.concatenate([node_ids, cp_ids, sp_ids])

    # 2. Gather Containers
    curve_ids = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]["curve_id"].to_numpy()
    surface_ids = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]["surface_id"].to_numpy()

    # 3. Enforce the mathematical bounds
    enforce_id_namespace(ids=all_rf_ids, bounds=GlobalIdNamespace.NODES)
    enforce_id_namespace(ids=curve_ids, bounds=GlobalIdNamespace.CURVES)
    enforce_id_namespace(ids=surface_ids, bounds=GlobalIdNamespace.SURFACES)


def _validate_asset_class_subclass_consistency(
    dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
) -> None:
    """asset_subclass must map to the declared asset_class for all 3 meta tables."""

    def _expected_class(subclass_val: str) -> str | None:
        try:
            return ASSET_SUBCLASS_TO_CLASS[AssetSubclass(subclass_val)].value
        except ConfigurationError:
            return None

    for sheet in ("node_meta", "curve_meta", "surface_meta"):
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet]
        if "asset_class" not in df.columns or "asset_subclass" not in df.columns:
            continue
        valid = df[["asset_class", "asset_subclass"]].dropna()
        if valid.empty:
            continue
        expected = valid["asset_subclass"].map(_expected_class)
        bad_mask = expected.notna() & (valid["asset_class"] != expected)
        if bad_mask.any():
            offending = valid.loc[bad_mask].copy()
            offending["expected_class"] = expected[bad_mask]
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"Domain violation: {sheet} has inconsistent "
                f"asset_class / asset_subclass.\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )


def _validate_fx_node_invariants(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    FX node contract:
      asset_class == "FX"  <=>  fx_base_ccy is non-null
      For FX nodes: node_name == fx_base_ccy + quote_ccy.
    """
    df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    is_fx = df["asset_class"] == AssetClass.FX.value
    has_base = df["fx_base_ccy"].notna()

    inconsistent = is_fx != has_base
    if inconsistent.any():
        offending = df.loc[
            inconsistent,
            ["node_name", "asset_class", "fx_base_ccy", "quote_ccy"],
        ]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Domain violation: asset_class=='FX' iff fx_base_ccy is non-null.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    fx_rows = df.loc[is_fx]
    if fx_rows.empty:
        return
    expected_names = fx_rows["fx_base_ccy"].astype(str) + fx_rows["quote_ccy"].astype(
        str
    )
    name_mismatch = fx_rows["node_name"] != expected_names
    if name_mismatch.any():
        offending = fx_rows.loc[
            name_mismatch, ["node_name", "fx_base_ccy", "quote_ccy"]
        ]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Domain violation: FX node_name must equal fx_base_ccy + quote_ccy.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # FX pairs are unique: (base, quote) appears at most once.
    composite = list(zip(fx_rows["fx_base_ccy"], fx_rows["quote_ccy"], strict=True))
    if len(set(composite)) != len(composite):
        from collections import Counter

        dupes = [k for k, c in Counter(composite).items() if c > 1]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"FX pair duplication: (base, quote) pairs appear more than once: {dupes}"
        )


def _validate_axis_roll_pairs(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    (XAxisKind, RollMethod) compatibility:

      - LABEL curves must have spot_roll_rule_name == NULL.
      - OFFSET_* curves must have spot_roll_rule_name populated.
      - The resolved (XAxisKind, RollMethod) pair must be in the
        ALLOWED_X_AXIS_KIND_ROLL_METHOD_PAIRS table.

    Vectorized: a single pass over each meta table, no row loops.
    """
    srr_df = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]
    rule_to_method: dict[str, str] = dict(
        zip(
            srr_df["rule_name"].astype(str),
            srr_df["roll_method"].astype(str),
            strict=True,
        )
    )

    # Pre-compute the allowed-pair set as plain strings (cheap O(K) build).
    allowed_pair_names: frozenset[tuple[str, str]] = frozenset(
        (xk.name, rm.name) for xk, rm in ALLOWED_X_AXIS_KIND_ROLL_METHOD_PAIRS
    )

    for sheet, name_col in (
        ("curve_meta", "curve_name"),
        ("surface_meta", "surface_name"),
    ):
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet]
        if df.empty:
            continue

        x_kind = df["x_axis_kind"].astype(str)
        rule = df["spot_roll_rule_name"]
        rule_populated = rule.notna()
        needs_roll = x_kind.isin(X_AXIS_KINDS_REQUIRING_ROLL)

        # Missing rule on OFFSET container.
        missing = needs_roll & ~rule_populated
        if missing.any():
            offending = df.loc[missing, [name_col, "x_axis_kind"]]
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"{sheet}: OFFSET containers missing spot_roll_rule_name:\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )

        # Populated rule on LABEL container.
        extraneous = ~needs_roll & rule_populated
        if extraneous.any():
            offending = df.loc[
                extraneous, [name_col, "x_axis_kind", "spot_roll_rule_name"]
            ]
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"{sheet}: LABEL containers with populated spot_roll_rule_name:\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )

        # For OFFSET rows, check the (x_kind, method) pair is allowed.
        offset_rows = df.loc[needs_roll].copy()
        if offset_rows.empty:
            continue
        # method = offset_rows["spot_roll_rule_name"].astype(str).map(rule_to_method)
        # pair_strs = list(
        #     zip(offset_rows["x_axis_kind"].astype(str), method, strict=True)
        # )
        # bad_mask = np.array(
        #     [p not in allowed_pair_names for p in pair_strs], dtype=bool
        # )
        method = offset_rows["spot_roll_rule_name"].astype(str).map(rule_to_method)
        # Vectorized pair check: encode each (x_kind, roll_method) tuple as a
        # single "X|M" string and isin() against the precomputed allowed set.
        # Drops the Python-level list comprehension in the validation hot path.
        joined = offset_rows["x_axis_kind"].astype(str) + "|" + method.astype(str)
        allowed_join = frozenset(f"{xk}|{rm}" for xk, rm in allowed_pair_names)
        bad_mask = ~joined.isin(allowed_join).to_numpy(copy=False)
        if bad_mask.any():
            offending = offset_rows.loc[
                bad_mask, [name_col, "x_axis_kind", "spot_roll_rule_name"]
            ].copy()
            offending["roll_method"] = method[bad_mask].values
            raise ConfigurationError(
                f"{sheet}: disallowed (x_axis_kind, roll_method) combinations:\n"
                f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
            )


def _validate_fh_bh_mmdd(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    fh_bh_mmdd = dict_of_dfs["spot_roll_rule"]["fh_bh_mmdd"]
    if fh_bh_mmdd.empty:
        return
    fh_bh_mmdd = fh_bh_mmdd.dropna().astype(str)

    # Assert correct length of 9 (e.g., 0401|1001)
    bad_length = fh_bh_mmdd.str.len() != 9
    if bad_length.any():
        offending = fh_bh_mmdd[bad_length]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Invalid fh_bh_mmdd length:\n{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Assert correct format of 4 digits, pipe, 4 digits.
    bad_format = ~fh_bh_mmdd.str.match(r"^\d{4}\|\d{4}$")
    if bad_format.any():
        offending = fh_bh_mmdd[bad_format]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Invalid fh_bh_mmdd format (expected 'MMDD|MMDD'):\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_curve_point_token_grammar(
    dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
) -> None:
    """
    Every curve_point.tenor_token matches its parent's x_axis_kind regex.

    Vectorized: group rows by parent x_axis_kind, apply pd.Series.str.match
    with the kind-specific regex to each group in one vectorized call.
    """
    cm_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    cp_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_point_meta"]
    if cp_df.empty:
        return

    # Join parent x_axis_kind onto each point row via map (vectorized).
    parent_kind_by_id = cm_df.set_index("curve_id")["x_axis_kind"].astype(str)
    work = cp_df[["curve_id", "rf_id", "tenor_token"]].copy()
    work["x_axis_kind"] = work["curve_id"].map(parent_kind_by_id)

    bad_pieces: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
    for kind_name, group in work.groupby("x_axis_kind"):
        # Explicitly cast kind_name to a string
        kind_name_str = str(kind_name)
        regex = X_AXIS_TOKEN_REGEX[XAxisKind[kind_name_str]]
        good = group["tenor_token"].astype(str).str.match(regex)
        bad = ~good
        if bad.any():
            bad_pieces.append(group.loc[bad])

    if bad_pieces:
        offending = pd.concat(bad_pieces, ignore_index=True)
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Curve point token grammar violations:\n"
            f"{offending.head(25).to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_surface_point_token_grammar(
    dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
) -> None:
    """
    Every surface_point.x_tenor_token matches its parent's x_axis_kind regex.

    Vectorized: same per-kind-group str.match pattern as the curve-point
    validator.
    """
    sm_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    sp_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_point_meta"]
    if sp_df.empty:
        return

    parent_kind_by_id = sm_df.set_index("surface_id")["x_axis_kind"].astype(str)
    work = sp_df[["surface_id", "rf_id", "x_tenor_token"]].copy()
    work["x_axis_kind"] = work["surface_id"].map(parent_kind_by_id)

    bad_pieces: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
    for kind_name, group in work.groupby("x_axis_kind"):
        # Explicitly cast kind_name to a string
        kind_name_str = str(kind_name)
        regex = X_AXIS_TOKEN_REGEX[XAxisKind[kind_name_str]]
        good = group["x_tenor_token"].astype(str).str.match(regex)
        bad = ~good
        if bad.any():
            bad_pieces.append(group.loc[bad])

    if bad_pieces:
        offending = pd.concat(bad_pieces, ignore_index=True)
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Surface point token grammar violations:\n"
            f"{offending.head(25).to_string(index=False)}"
        )


def _validate_discount_invariants(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    Discount-curve rules:
      - is_default_discount_for_ccy=True curves must have a real basis
        (not NOT_A_DISCOUNT_CURVE).
      - Non-discount curves (basis=NOT_A_DISCOUNT_CURVE) cannot be default.
      - Discount curves must have asset_class=='IR'.
      - The default's basis must equal FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS.
      - Exactly one default per currency that has any discount curve.
      - (ccy, basis) is unique among discount curves.
      - Every currency in the ccy table that appears as a quote_ccy on
        any other container must have at least one discount curve.
    """
    curve_df = dict_of_dfs["curve_meta"]
    node_df = dict_of_dfs["node_meta"]
    surf_df = dict_of_dfs["surface_meta"]
    # ccy_df = dict_of_dfs["ccy"]

    is_disc = curve_df["discount_basis"] != DiscountBasis.NOT_A_DISCOUNT_CURVE.name
    is_default = curve_df["is_default_discount_for_ccy"].astype(bool)

    # Default => is a real discount curve.
    bad = is_default & ~is_disc
    if bad.any():
        offending = curve_df.loc[bad, ["curve_name", "discount_basis"]]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Default discount flag set on non-discount curve(s):\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Discount curves must be IR.
    bad_class = is_disc & (curve_df["asset_class"] != AssetClass.IR.value)
    if bad_class.any():
        offending = curve_df.loc[bad_class, ["curve_name", "asset_class"]]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Discount curves must have asset_class=='IR'.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Default basis must equal the firm standard.
    bad_basis = is_default & (
        curve_df["discount_basis"] != FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS.name
    )
    if bad_basis.any():
        offending = curve_df.loc[bad_basis, ["curve_name", "discount_basis"]]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Default discount curves must use the firm-standard basis "
            f"{FIRM_DEFAULT_DISCOUNT_BASIS.name!r}.\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Exactly one default per currency that has any discount curve.
    disc_curves = curve_df.loc[
        is_disc,
        ["curve_name", "quote_ccy", "discount_basis", "is_default_discount_for_ccy"],
    ]
    default_counts = (
        disc_curves.groupby("quote_ccy")["is_default_discount_for_ccy"]
        .sum()
        .astype(int)
    )
    bad_ccys = default_counts[default_counts != 1]
    if not bad_ccys.empty:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Each currency with a discount curve must flag exactly one as "
            f"default. Currencies with !=1 default:\n"
            f"{bad_ccys.to_string()}"
        )

    # (ccy, basis) is unique among discount curves.
    composite = disc_curves[["quote_ccy", "discount_basis"]]
    dupe_mask = composite.duplicated(keep=False)
    if dupe_mask.any():
        offending = disc_curves.loc[dupe_mask]
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Duplicate (quote_ccy, discount_basis) among discount curves:\n"
            f"{offending.to_string(index=False)}"
        )

    # Coverage: every currency that's a quote_ccy somewhere must have a
    # discount curve. (Skips currencies that exist in `ccy` but aren't used.)
    quote_ccys_used = pd.Index(
        pd.concat([curve_df["quote_ccy"], surf_df["quote_ccy"], node_df["quote_ccy"]])
        .dropna()
        .unique()
    )
    ccys_with_discount = set(disc_curves["quote_ccy"].astype(str))
    missing = sorted(set(quote_ccys_used.astype(str)) - ccys_with_discount)
    if missing:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Currencies with no discount curve: {missing}. "
            f"Every active quote_ccy must have at least one discount curve."
        )

    # Sanity: discount_basis used by discount curves must be one of the real bases.
    real_bases = set(disc_curves["discount_basis"].astype(str))
    bogus = real_bases - DISCOUNT_BASES_EXCLUDING_SENTINEL
    if bogus:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Unexpected discount_basis on discount curves: {sorted(bogus)}. "
            f"Allowed: {sorted(DISCOUNT_BASES_EXCLUDING_SENTINEL)}"
        )


def _validate_no_blanks(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """Hard fail on empty / whitespace-only strings in non-nullable string cols."""
    string_cols_by_sheet: dict[str, dict[str, bool]] = {
        "ccy": {"ccy_name": False},
        "node_meta": {
            "node_name": False,
            "quote_ccy": False,
            "asset_class": False,
            "asset_subclass": False,
            "unit_type": False,
            "unit_scale": False,
            "shift_type": False,
            "fx_base_ccy": True,
        },
        "curve_meta": {
            "curve_name": False,
            "quote_ccy": False,
            "asset_class": False,
            "asset_subclass": False,
            "unit_type": False,
            "unit_scale": False,
            "shift_type": False,
            "x_axis_kind": False,
            "x_interp_method": False,
            "x_extrap_method": False,
            "spot_roll_rule_name": True,
            "discount_basis": False,
        },
        "curve_point_meta": {"tenor_token": False},
        "surface_meta": {
            "surface_name": False,
            "quote_ccy": False,
            "asset_class": False,
            "asset_subclass": False,
            "unit_type": False,
            "unit_scale": False,
            "shift_type": False,
            "x_axis_kind": False,
            "x_interp_method": False,
            "x_extrap_method": False,
            "spot_roll_rule_name": True,
            "y_axis_kind": False,
            "y_interp_method": False,
            "y_extrap_method": False,
        },
        "surface_point_meta": {"x_tenor_token": False, "y_label": False},
        "spot_roll_rule": {
            "rule_name": False,
            "roll_method": False,
            "fh_bh_mmdd": True,
        },
    }
    for sheet_name, cols in string_cols_by_sheet.items():
        df = dict_of_dfs[sheet_name]
        for col, nullable in cols.items():
            _check_no_blank_strings(df, col, sheet_name, nullable=nullable)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def validate_static_config_dfs(dict_of_dfs: dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> None:
    """
    Execute all validation tiers against the loaded config DataFrames.

    Raises ConfigurationError on the first violation with a descriptive message.
    """
    _check_required_sheets(dict_of_dfs)

    expected_columns: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {
        "ccy": frozenset({"ccy_name", "ccy_id"}),
        "holiday_calendars": frozenset({"holiday_calendar_name", "holiday_date"}),
        "spot_roll_rule": frozenset(
            {
                "rule_name",
                "roll_method",
                "offset_in_tenor_units",
                "roll_month",
                "roll_day",
                "fh_bh_mmdd",
                "post_roll_shift",
            }
        ),
        "node_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "node_id",
                "node_name",
                "quote_ccy",
                "asset_class",
                "asset_subclass",
                "unit_type",
                "unit_scale",
                "shift_type",
                "fx_base_ccy",
            }
        ),
        "curve_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "curve_id",
                "curve_name",
                "quote_ccy",
                "asset_class",
                "asset_subclass",
                "unit_type",
                "unit_scale",
                "shift_type",
                "x_axis_kind",
                "x_interp_method",
                "x_extrap_method",
                "spot_roll_rule_name",
                "discount_basis",
                "is_default_discount_for_ccy",
            }
        ),
        "curve_point_meta": frozenset({"rf_id", "curve_id", "tenor_token"}),
        "surface_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "surface_id",
                "surface_name",
                "quote_ccy",
                "asset_class",
                "asset_subclass",
                "unit_type",
                "unit_scale",
                "shift_type",
                "x_axis_kind",
                "x_interp_method",
                "x_extrap_method",
                "spot_roll_rule_name",
                "y_axis_kind",
                "y_interp_method",
                "y_extrap_method",
            }
        ),
        "surface_point_meta": frozenset(
            {
                "rf_id",
                "surface_id",
                "x_tenor_token",
                "y_label",
            }
        ),
    }
    for sheet_name, cols in expected_columns.items():
        _check_columns(dict_of_dfs[sheet_name], cols, sheet_name)

    _validate_no_blanks(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_enums(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_fks(dict_of_dfs)

    # Tier 5 — domain
    _validate_rf_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_container_id_uniqueness(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_global_id_namespace(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_asset_class_subclass_consistency(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_fx_node_invariants(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_axis_roll_pairs(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_curve_point_token_grammar(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_surface_point_token_grammar(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_discount_invariants(dict_of_dfs)
    _validate_fh_bh_mmdd(dict_of_dfs)
```

```python
# src/riskquant/constants.py

"""
Common constants used across the riskquant codebase.

This module separates:
- typed sentinel values used in numeric payloads / arrays
- general configuration constants used by the portfolio engine
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# Typed sentinel values for payload fields
# ==============================================================================

# Universal sentinel for "no valid ID".
# Used across instrument_ids, underlying_ids, volatility_ids, and discount_curve_ids.
NO_ID = np.int32(-999_999)

# Universal sentinel for missing dates (settlement, delivery, expiry).
# Using -1 allows for clean `date_ord >= 0` masking.
NO_DATE_ORD32 = np.int32(-99_999)

# Specific sentinel for FX: No conversion required (Underlying CCY == Settlement CCY)
NO_FX_RF_REQUIRED = np.int32(-1)

# Invalid UIX sentinel for any universe index (*_uix fields).
# Used exclusively in the Execution Engine (blocks/compilers), never in Ingest.
INVALID_UIX = np.int32(-999)

# NOT IMPLEMENTED sentinels for fields expected to be populated but aren't yet.
NOT_IMPLEMENTED_SENTINEL: np.int8 = np.int8(-99)

# ==============================================================================
# Option encoding
# ==============================================================================

# 1 for call options, -1 for put options, 0 for not-an-option.
# Allows for branchless payoff calculation: payoff * cp_mult
CALL_OPTION_MULT = np.int8(1)
PUT_OPTION_MULT = np.int8(-1)
NOT_OPTION_MULT = np.int8(0)

# ==============================================================================
# Date encoding
# ==============================================================================

DATE_EPOCH: date = date(1970, 1, 1)

# ==============================================================================
# Numeric configuration
# ==============================================================================

ROUND_DECIMALS_LEVEL = 8
ROUND_DECIMALS_QUANTITY = 8
ROUND_DECIMALS_STRIKE_PRICE = 8

POSITION_DUST_TOLERANCE = 1e-8
PD_DUST_TOLERANCE = 1e-10

# ============================================
# --- DEVELOPMENT-ONLY FALLBACK VALUES
# ============================================

FALLBACK_MOCK_VOLATILITY_DO_NOT_SHIP: float = 0.50

# ==============================================================================
# --- EWMA Volatility Calculation Constants
# ==============================================================================

EWMA_DECAY_FACTOR: float = 0.94
MAX_EWMA_VOL_RATIO: float = 10.0
MIN_EWMA_VARIANCE: float = 1e-12

# ==============================================================================
# --- Credit Risk Constants
# ==============================================================================

NO_PARENT_OBLIGOR: np.int64 = np.int64(-1)
NO_RATING_ID: np.int8 = np.int8(0)
NO_SECTOR_ID: np.int32 = np.int32(-1)
NO_COUNTRY_ID: np.int32 = np.int32(-1)

# ==============================================================================
# RANDOM SEED FOR TESTING
# ==============================================================================

RNG_SEED: int = 42
```

```python
# riskquant/exceptions.py


class PrettyKeyError(KeyError):
    """
    KeyError variant that prints the message using str(...) instead of repr(...),
    allowing multi-line, human-readable diagnostics while preserving KeyError
    semantics.
    """

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return str(self.args[0])


class RiskQuantError(Exception):
    """Base exception for all custom riskquant errors."""

    pass


class ConfigurationError(RiskQuantError):
    """
    Raised when static configuration, ID namespaces, or
    market data mappings violate the engine's strict contracts.
    """

    pass
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/config/risk_proxy_config/canonical_risk_proxy_config.py

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import RiskProxyConfigData
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import validate_column_lengths

# All names referenced below exist in the canonical static_config fixture
# (see ingest_fixtures/config/risk_static_config/_parts/*).
#
# Single-hop policy (enforced by validate_proxy_maps):
#   - No proxy_name appears as a required_name anywhere in this table.
#   - No required_name proxies itself.
#   - No container in container-level rows has a child rf in rf-level rows.
#
# Container-level rows (3):
#   WTI_CRUDE_FWD          -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD     (oil-on-oil, scale 0.95)
#   HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD      -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD     (gas-on-oil, scale 1.40, illustrative)
#   POWER_CH_BASELOAD_HY   -> CO2_EUA_ANNUAL_FWD  (illustrative, scale 1.20)
#
# RF-level rows (3):
#   USDGBP                 -> USDEUR              (G10 cross via parent, scale 0.85)
#   EURCHF                 -> USDEUR              (low-vol cross via parent, scale 0.60)
#   USD_LIBOR_3M[10Y]      -> USD_SOFR_OIS[10Y]   (tenor-basis substitution, scale 1.00)
#
# Cross-level check: WTI_CRUDE_FWD, HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD, POWER_CH_BASELOAD_HY
# are container-proxied. None of their child rf names appear as
# rf-level required_names below — no conflict.


def _get_data() -> RiskProxyConfigData:
    return {
        "proxying_level": [
            "CONTAINER",
            "CONTAINER",
            "CONTAINER",
            "RISK_FACTOR",
            "RISK_FACTOR",
            "RISK_FACTOR",
        ],
        "required_name": [
            "WTI_CRUDE_FWD",
            "HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD",
            "POWER_CH_BASELOAD_HY",
            "USDGBP",
            "EURCHF",
            "USD_LIBOR_3M[10Y]",
        ],
        "proxy_name": [
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD",
            "CO2_EUA_ANNUAL_FWD",
            "USDEUR",
            "USDEUR",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS[10Y]",
        ],
        "vol_scaling_factor": [
            0.95,
            1.40,
            1.20,
            0.85,
            0.60,
            1.00,
        ],
    }


CANONICAL_RISK_PROXY_CONFIG_DATA: RiskProxyConfigData = _get_data()


validate_column_lengths(CANONICAL_RISK_PROXY_CONFIG_DATA)


CANONICAL_RISK_PROXY_CONFIG_DF = pd.DataFrame(CANONICAL_RISK_PROXY_CONFIG_DATA)


def _debug_print() -> None:
    from riskquant.utils.printer import (
        print_calling_file,
        print_df,
        print_dict,
        print_success_message,
    )

    print_calling_file()

    print("Canonical Risk Proxy Config Data:")
    print_dict(CANONICAL_RISK_PROXY_CONFIG_DATA)

    print("\nAs DataFrame:")
    print_df(CANONICAL_RISK_PROXY_CONFIG_DF, index=False, float_format="{:.2f}")

    print_success_message(add_space_before=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _debug_print()
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

        "ccy_name": CANONICAL_CCY_NAME,
        "ccy_id": CANONICAL_CCY_ID,

        print_calling_file,
        print_df,
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/config/risk_static_config/_parts/curve_point_meta.py

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import RiskStaticConfigCurvePointMeta
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import validate_column_lengths

# rf_id allocation per curve. Each curve gets a contiguous block; the
# first rf_id of the block is `_rf_id_base`, and points are emitted in
# the order their tenor_tokens appear in `_tenor_tokens`.
#
# rf_id partition (must not collide with node_meta or surface_point_meta):
#   nodes          : 0 ... 99_999
#   curve_points   : 100_000 ... 199_999
#   surface_points : 200_000 ... 299_999
#
# Tenor labels follow the IR conventional schedule: [ON]..[10Y].
# Monthly commodities use OFFSET_M tokens [M+k]; annual carbon uses
# OFFSET_Y tokens [Y+k]; half-year power uses OFFSET_HY tokens [HY+k].

_LABEL_TENORS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "[ON]",
    "[1W]",
    "[1M]",
    "[3M]",
    "[6M]",
    "[1Y]",
    "[2Y]",
    "[5Y]",
    "[10Y]",
)

_MONTHLY_OFFSETS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "[M+0]",
    "[M+1]",
    "[M+2]",
    "[M+3]",
    "[M+6]",
    "[M+12]",
)

_ANNUAL_OFFSETS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "[Y+0]",
    "[Y+1]",
    "[Y+2]",
    "[Y+3]",
)

_HALF_YEAR_OFFSETS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "[HY+0]",
    "[HY+1]",
    "[HY+2]",
    "[HY+3]",
)


# (curve_id, rf_id_base, tenor_tokens)
_CURVE_BLOCKS: tuple[tuple[int, int, tuple[str, ...]], ...] = (
    # --- USD discount curves ---
    (100_000_000, 1000, _LABEL_TENORS),  # USD_SOFR_OIS
    (100_000_001, 1010, _LABEL_TENORS),  # USD_LIBOR_3M
    (100_000_002, 1020, _LABEL_TENORS),  # USD_CSA_EUR
    # --- EUR discount curves ---
    (100_000_003, 1100, _LABEL_TENORS),  # EUR_ESTR_OIS
    (100_000_004, 1110, _LABEL_TENORS),  # EUR_EURIBOR_6M
    # --- GBP discount curves ---
    (100_000_005, 1200, _LABEL_TENORS),  # GBP_SONIA_OIS
    # --- CHF discount curves ---
    (100_000_006, 1300, _LABEL_TENORS),  # CHF_SARON_OIS
    # --- AUD discount curves ---
    (100_000_007, 1400, _LABEL_TENORS),  # AUD_AONIA_OIS
    # --- Commodity forward curves ---
    (100_000_008, 2000, _MONTHLY_OFFSETS),  # BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
    (100_000_009, 2010, _MONTHLY_OFFSETS),  # WTI_CRUDE_FWD
    (100_000_010, 2020, _MONTHLY_OFFSETS),  # HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD
    (100_000_011, 2100, _ANNUAL_OFFSETS),  # CO2_EUA_ANNUAL_FWD
    (100_000_012, 2200, _HALF_YEAR_OFFSETS),  # POWER_CH_BASELOAD_HY
)


def _get_data() -> RiskStaticConfigCurvePointMeta:
    rf_ids: list[int] = []
    curve_ids: list[int] = []
    tenor_tokens: list[str] = []

    for curve_id, rf_id_base, tokens in _CURVE_BLOCKS:
        for offset, token in enumerate(tokens):
            rf_ids.append(rf_id_base + offset)
            curve_ids.append(curve_id)
            tenor_tokens.append(token)

    return {
        "rf_id": rf_ids,
        "curve_id": curve_ids,
        "tenor_token": tenor_tokens,
    }


CANONICAL_CURVE_POINT_META_DATA: RiskStaticConfigCurvePointMeta = _get_data()

validate_column_lengths(CANONICAL_CURVE_POINT_META_DATA)

_CANONICAL_CURVE_POINT_META_DF = pd.DataFrame(CANONICAL_CURVE_POINT_META_DATA)


def _debug_print() -> None:
    from riskquant.utils.printer import (
        print_calling_file,
        print_df,
        print_dict,
        print_success_message,
    )

    print_calling_file()

    print(
        f"Canonical Curve Point Meta Data ({len(CANONICAL_CURVE_POINT_META_DATA['rf_id'])} rows):"
    )
    print_dict(CANONICAL_CURVE_POINT_META_DATA)

    print("As DataFrame:")
    print_df(_CANONICAL_CURVE_POINT_META_DF, index=False)

    print_success_message(add_space_before=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _debug_print()
```
</file>

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import RiskStaticConfigHolidayCalendar
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import validate_column_lengths

# Five named calendars covering AUD, CHF, EUR, GBP, USD, plus the
# Eurozone TARGET2 settlement calendar (named EUR). Each entry includes
# the year-end pair plus at least one mid-year holiday to exercise the
# business-day engine across the trading year.

_CALENDAR_ENTRIES: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
    # --- USD (Federal Reserve calendar; subset) ---
    ("US", "2026-01-01"),  # New Year's Day
    ("US", "2026-07-03"),  # Independence Day (observed)
    ("US", "2026-11-26"),  # Thanksgiving
    ("US", "2026-12-25"),  # Christmas Day
    ("US", "2027-01-01"),
    ("US", "2027-07-05"),
    ("US", "2027-11-25"),
    ("US", "2027-12-24"),

    # --- GBP (Bank of England subset) ---
    ("GB", "2026-01-01"),
    ("GB", "2026-05-04"),  # Early May bank holiday
    ("GB", "2026-08-31"),  # Summer bank holiday
    ("GB", "2026-12-25"),
    ("GB", "2026-12-28"),  # Boxing Day (observed)
    ("GB", "2027-01-01"),
    ("GB", "2027-05-03"),
    ("GB", "2027-08-30"),
    ("GB", "2027-12-27"),
    ("GB", "2027-12-28"),

    # --- CHF (Zurich SIX) ---
    ("CH", "2026-01-01"),
    ("CH", "2026-01-02"),  # Berchtoldstag
    ("CH", "2026-08-03"),  # Swiss National Day (observed)
    ("CH", "2026-12-25"),
    ("CH", "2026-12-28"),  # Stephen (observed)
    ("CH", "2027-01-01"),
    ("CH", "2027-08-02"),
    ("CH", "2027-12-27"),

    ("AU", "2026-04-27"),  # ANZAC Day (observed)
    ("AU", "2026-12-25"),
    ("AU", "2026-12-28"),  # Boxing Day (observed)
    ("AU", "2027-01-01"),
    ("AU", "2027-01-26"),
    ("AU", "2027-04-26"),

    # --- EUR (TARGET2 — settlement calendar for euro-area) ---

    ("EUR", "2026-05-01"),  # Labour Day
    ("EUR", "2026-12-25"),
    ("EUR", "2026-12-28"),
    ("EUR", "2027-01-01"),
    ("EUR", "2027-05-03"),
    ("EUR", "2027-12-27"),
    ("EUR", "2027-12-28"),
]

        "holiday_calendar_name": list(names),
        "holiday_date":          list(dates),
    }
```

```python
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import validate_column_lengths

# Three vol surfaces, one per asset class that supports vanilla options.

#                          OTC FX vol convention; delta-quoted smile.

#                          forward terms.

        "surface_id": list(_CANONICAL_SURFACE_IDS),
        "surface_name": CANONICAL_SURFACE_NAMES,
        "quote_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "USD",  # FX vol surface in standard pair quote convention
            "USD",

        "asset_class": [
            "COMMODITY",
            "FX",
            "EQUITY",
        ],
        "asset_subclass": [
            "CM_OIL",
            "FX_G10",
            "EQ_SINGLE_NAME",
        ],
        "unit_type": [
            "VOL",
            "VOL",
            "VOL",
        ],
        "unit_scale": [
            "DECIMAL",
            "DECIMAL",
            "DECIMAL",
        ],
        "shift_type": [
            "LOG_RETURN",
            "LOG_RETURN",
            "LOG_RETURN",
        ],
        "x_axis_kind": [
            "OFFSET_M",
            "LABEL",
            "LABEL",
        ],
        "x_interp_method": [
            "LINEAR",
            "LINEAR",
            "LINEAR",
        ],
        "x_extrap_method": [
            "FLAT",
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/config/risk_static_config/_parts/surface_point_meta.py

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import validate_column_lengths

# rf_id partition (must not collide with node_meta or curve_point_meta):
#   nodes          : 1..99
#   curve_points   : 1_000..9_999
#   surface_points : 10_000..99_999
#
# Each surface is a 4-expiry x 5-y-label grid (20 points per surface).
# Total surface points: 3 surfaces x 20 = 60.
#
# y_label is stored verbatim (case-preserving) in surface_point_meta;
# the validator does not uppercase y labels since strike values are
# numeric-typed strings ("50.00", "75.50") that the surface interpolator
# parses at runtime.

# (surface_id, rf_id_base, x_tokens, y_labels)
_SURFACE_BLOCKS: tuple[tuple[int, int, tuple[str, ...], tuple[str, ...]], ...] = (
    # --- BRENT_VOL_SURF: 4 monthly expiries x 5 absolute strikes ---
    (
        200_000_000,
        10_000,
        ("[M+0]", "[M+1]", "[M+3]", "[M+6]"),
        ("50.00", "65.00", "75.00", "85.00", "100.00"),
    ),
    # --- USDEUR_VOL_SURF: 4 LABEL expiries x 5 delta points ---
    (
        200_000_001,
        11_000,
        ("[1W]", "[1M]", "[3M]", "[1Y]"),
        ("0.10", "0.25", "0.50", "0.75", "0.90"),
    ),
    # --- AAPL_VOL_SURF: 4 LABEL expiries x 5 log-moneyness levels ---
    (
        200_000_002,
        12_000,
        ("[1M]", "[3M]", "[6M]", "[1Y]"),
        ("-0.20", "-0.10", "0.00", "0.10", "0.20"),
    ),
)


def _get_data() -> RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta:
    rf_ids: list[int] = []
    surface_ids: list[int] = []
    x_tokens: list[str] = []
    y_labels: list[str] = []

    for surface_id, rf_id_base, xs, ys in _SURFACE_BLOCKS:
        idx = 0
        for x_token in xs:
            for y_label in ys:
                rf_ids.append(rf_id_base + idx)
                surface_ids.append(surface_id)
                x_tokens.append(x_token)
                y_labels.append(y_label)
                idx += 1

    return {
        "rf_id": rf_ids,
        "surface_id": surface_ids,
        "x_tenor_token": x_tokens,
        "y_label": y_labels,
    }


CANONICAL_SURFACE_POINT_META_DATA: RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta = _get_data()

validate_column_lengths(CANONICAL_SURFACE_POINT_META_DATA)

_CANONICAL_SURFACE_POINT_META_DF = pd.DataFrame(CANONICAL_SURFACE_POINT_META_DATA)


def _debug_print() -> None:
    from riskquant.utils.printer import (
        print_calling_file,
        print_df,
        print_dict,
        print_success_message,
    )

    print_calling_file()

    print(
        f"Canonical Surface Point Meta Data "
        f"({len(CANONICAL_SURFACE_POINT_META_DATA['rf_id'])} rows):"
    )
    print_dict(CANONICAL_SURFACE_POINT_META_DATA)

    print("As DataFrame:")
    print_df(_CANONICAL_SURFACE_POINT_META_DF, index=False)

    print_success_message(add_space_before=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _debug_print()
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/constants.py

"""
Constants used across the ingest fixtures codebase.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Final

import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# RANDOM NUMBER GENERATION
# ==============================================================================

# Seed for RNG in ingest fixtures. This is a constant to ensure reproducibility of
# any random data generated for testing purposes.
CANONICAL_RNG_SEED: Final[int] = 49

# ==============================================================================
# BUSINESS DAY CALCULATIONS
# ==============================================================================

# Business days per year for Brownian-motion scaling.
BUSINESS_DAYS_PER_YEAR: Final[int] = 252

# Number of business days of history to synthesize.
CANONICAL_HISTORY_BUSINESS_DAYS: Final[int] = 260


# ==============================================================================
# GLOBAL HISTORICAL SIMULATION CONSTANTS
# ==============================================================================


# Output dtype for the synthetic history. float32 matches the downstream
# RiskFactorMatrix default; float64 would be wasted precision.
HISTORY_DTYPE: Final[type[np.floating]] = np.float32


# ==============================================================================
# MARKET RISK FACTOR SIMULATION CONSTANTS
# ==============================================================================


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class _MarketRiskFactorParams:
    """Per-factor GBM/ABM parameters used to seed the synthetic history."""

    s0: float
    annualized_vol: float
    is_rate: bool  # True -> arithmetic BM; False -> geometric BM


# Defaults applied per asset class / unit type. Keep them in one block so
# tuning is obvious and no values are buried inside the universe loop.
EQUITY_DEFAULTS: Final[_MarketRiskFactorParams] = _MarketRiskFactorParams(
    s0=185.50,
    annualized_vol=0.25,
    is_rate=False,
)
FX_DEFAULTS: Final[_MarketRiskFactorParams] = _MarketRiskFactorParams(
    s0=0.9250,
    annualized_vol=0.08,
    is_rate=False,
)
RATE_DEFAULTS: Final[_MarketRiskFactorParams] = _MarketRiskFactorParams(
    s0=4.00,
    annualized_vol=0.0075,
    is_rate=True,
)
GAS_COMMODITY_DEFAULTS: Final[_MarketRiskFactorParams] = _MarketRiskFactorParams(
    s0=2.50,
    annualized_vol=0.35,
    is_rate=False,
)
OIL_COMMODITY_DEFAULTS: Final[_MarketRiskFactorParams] = _MarketRiskFactorParams(
    s0=80.00,
    annualized_vol=0.35,
    is_rate=False,
)
VOL_OF_VOL_DEFAULTS: Final[_MarketRiskFactorParams] = _MarketRiskFactorParams(
    s0=0.35,
    annualized_vol=0.50,
    is_rate=False,
)
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/credit_exposure/minimal_spot_with_netting_sets.py
"""
Canonical market-risk portfolio for the credit-exposure pipeline.

Eight SPOT trades against the canonical static-config underlyings:
- Six are linked to one of three netting sets (101, 102, 103); two carry
  no netting linkage and are excluded from the exposure path by
  `compile_exposure`.
- Quantities are mixed-sign so the RC kernel exercises both the V > C
  branch (set 101 nets long) and the RC = 0 floor (set 102 nets short).

This is a *market-risk* ingest frame — type `MarketRiskIngestData` —
that happens to carry the `netting_set_id` linkage column. Replacement-
cost-specific parameters live on the netting-set master
(`net_collateral`); nothing model-specific is on the trade rows.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import MarketRiskIngestData
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import validate_column_lengths
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import Instrument


def _get_data() -> MarketRiskIngestData:
    return {
        "uid": [1001, 1002, 1003, 1004, 1005, 1006, 1007, 1008],
        "instrument": [Instrument.SPOT.name] * 8,
        # Mixed signs so some netting sets net negative -> RC floors to 0.
        "quantity": [
            10.0,  # 1.  NS 101
            -4.0,  # 2.  NS 101
            6.0,  # 3.  NS 101  -> set 101 nets long
            8.0,  # 4.  NS 102
            -9.0,  # 5.  NS 102  -> set 102 nets short, RC = 0
            5.0,  # 6.  NS 103  -> single-trade netting set
            3.0,  # 7.  no netting set -> excluded from exposure
            -2.0,  # 8.  no netting set -> excluded from exposure
        ],
        # Curve-point relative name from the canonical static config.
        # All eight rows point at the same risk factor so the exposure
        # path resolves them to a single source.
        "underlying": ["BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]"] * 8,
        "settlement_ccy": ["USD"] * 8,
        # Effective-netting-set linkage. None -> NO_NETTING_SET (dropped
        # by compile_exposure). Trades 1-3 share 101, 4-5 share 102,
        # 6 is alone, 7-8 carry no linkage.
        "netting_set_id": [101, 101, 101, 102, 102, 103, None, None],
        # Irrelevant for SPOT.
        "strike_price": [None] * 8,
        "strike_ccy": [None] * 8,
        "is_call": [None] * 8,
        "expiry_date": [None] * 8,
        "delivery_date": [None] * 8,
        "settlement_date": [None] * 8,
        "under_discount_curve": [None] * 8,
        "strike_discount_curve": [None] * 8,
    }


CANONICAL_CREDIT_EXPOSURE_INGEST_DATA: MarketRiskIngestData = _get_data()

validate_column_lengths(CANONICAL_CREDIT_EXPOSURE_INGEST_DATA)

CANONICAL_CREDIT_EXPOSURE_INGEST_DF = pd.DataFrame(
    CANONICAL_CREDIT_EXPOSURE_INGEST_DATA
)


def _debug_print() -> None:
    from riskquant.utils.printer import (
        print_calling_file,
        print_df,
        print_dict,
        print_success_message,
    )

    print_calling_file()

    print_dict(
        CANONICAL_CREDIT_EXPOSURE_INGEST_DATA,
        title="Canonical Credit-Exposure Ingest Data (SPOT + netting sets)",
    )

    print_df(
        CANONICAL_CREDIT_EXPOSURE_INGEST_DF,
        title="Canonical Credit-Exposure Ingest Data as DataFrame (SPOT + netting sets)",
    )

    print_success_message(add_space_before=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _debug_print()
```

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\ingest_fixtures\credit_factors\minimal_factor_data.py">
```python
# Refactored canonical credit factor universe
# Key changes:
# 1. CHI factors generated independently via chi-square (correct marginal)
# 2. No volatility scaling applied to CHI factors
# 3. Clear separation between credit returns and chi mixing variables

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Final

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt
import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.as_of import CANONICAL_AS_OF_DATE
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.credit_factors.minimal_factor_universe import (
    CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_COUNT,
    CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX,
    CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAMES,
    CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_COLUMNS,
    CANONICAL_T_COPULA_NU_BY_CHI_FACTOR,
)
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.rng import get_canonical_rng

_logger: logging.Logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_rng: np.random.Generator = get_canonical_rng()

# ==============================================================================
# INDICES
# ==============================================================================

_GLOBAL: Final[int] = CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX["GLOBAL_CREDIT"]
_IDIO_1: Final[int] = CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX["IDIO_1"]
_IDIO_2: Final[int] = CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX["IDIO_2"]
_IDIO_3: Final[int] = CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX["IDIO_3"]

# ==============================================================================
# CORRELATION (CREDIT BLOCK ONLY)
# ==============================================================================

CREDIT_FACTOR_CORRELATION: Final[npt.NDArray[np.float64]] = np.array(
    [
        [1.00, 0.35, 0.30, 0.25],
        [0.35, 1.00, 0.10, 0.05],
        [0.30, 0.10, 1.00, 0.10],
        [0.25, 0.05, 0.10, 1.00],
    ],
    dtype=np.float64,
)

CREDIT_CHOLESKY: Final[npt.NDArray[np.float64]] = np.asarray(
    np.linalg.cholesky(CREDIT_FACTOR_CORRELATION),
    dtype=np.float64,
)

# ==============================================================================
# VOLS (CREDIT ONLY)
# ==============================================================================

CREDIT_FACTOR_VOLS: Final[npt.NDArray[np.float64]] = _rng.uniform(
    low=0.10,
    high=0.50,
    size=4,
).round(2)

# ==============================================================================
# TIME SERIES SETUP
# ==============================================================================

T: Final[int] = 260
DATES: Final[npt.NDArray[np.datetime64]] = np.sort(
    np.busday_offset(CANONICAL_AS_OF_DATE, -np.arange(T - 1, -1, -1))
)

# ==============================================================================
# GENERATE CREDIT FACTORS (GAUSSIAN)
# ==============================================================================

_independent_credit = _rng.standard_normal((T, 4))
_credit_returns = (_independent_credit @ CREDIT_CHOLESKY.T) * CREDIT_FACTOR_VOLS

# ==============================================================================
# GENERATE CHI FACTORS (TRUE CHI-SQUARE, INDEPENDENT)
# ==============================================================================

_chi_factors = np.column_stack(
    [_rng.chisquare(df=nu, size=T) for nu in CANONICAL_T_COPULA_NU_BY_CHI_FACTOR]
)

# ==============================================================================
# COMBINE INTO FULL ARRAY (ORDERED BY UIX)
# ==============================================================================

_full_array = np.zeros((T, CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_COUNT), dtype=np.float64)

# fill credit
for i, uix in enumerate([_GLOBAL, _IDIO_1, _IDIO_2, _IDIO_3]):
    _full_array[:, uix] = _credit_returns[:, i]

# fill chi
for i, uix in enumerate(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_COLUMNS):
    _full_array[:, uix] = _chi_factors[:, i]

# ==============================================================================
# DATAFRAMES (EXPLICIT SEMANTICS)
# ==============================================================================

CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF: Final[pd.DataFrame] = pd.DataFrame(
    _credit_returns,
    index=DATES,
    columns=["GLOBAL_CREDIT", "IDIO_1", "IDIO_2", "IDIO_3"],
)

CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF: Final[pd.DataFrame] = pd.DataFrame(
    _chi_factors,
    index=DATES,
    columns=[
        CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAMES[uix] for uix in CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_COLUMNS
    ],
)

FULL_FACTOR_DF: Final[pd.DataFrame] = pd.DataFrame(
    _full_array,
    index=DATES,
    columns=CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAMES,
)

# ==============================================================================
# VALIDATION
# ==============================================================================


def _validate_chi_independence() -> None:
    corr = np.corrcoef(_full_array, rowvar=False)

    chi_set = set(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_COLUMNS.tolist())
    # credit_set = set(range(CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_COUNT)) - chi_set

    for chi in chi_set:
        for other in range(CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_COUNT):
            if other == chi:
                continue
            val = corr[chi, other]
            if abs(val) > 0.15:
                raise AssertionError(
                    f"CHI factor correlation leakage: {chi} vs {other} = {val:.4f}"
                )

    _logger.info("CHI independence validation passed.")


def _validate_psd() -> None:
    eig = np.linalg.eigvalsh(CREDIT_FACTOR_CORRELATION)
    min_eig = float(eig.min())

    if min_eig < -1e-12:
        raise AssertionError("Credit correlation not PSD")

    cond = float(eig.max() / min_eig) if min_eig > 1e-12 else float("inf")

    _logger.info(f"PSD OK (min eig={min_eig:.6f}, cond={cond:.1f})")


_validate_psd()
_validate_chi_independence()

# ==============================================================================
# EXPORTS
# ==============================================================================

CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_TIME_SERIES_DF = FULL_FACTOR_DF

# ==============================================================================
# DEBUGGING / DIAGNOSTICS
# ==============================================================================


def _debug_print() -> None:
    print("=============================")
    print("=== CREDIT FACTOR RETURNS ===")
    print("=============================")
    print(CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF.tail(20))

    print("")
    print("=================================")
    print("=== CHI MIXING VARIABLES (χ²) ===")
    print("=================================")
    print(CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF.tail(20))

    print("")
    print("======================")
    print("=== FULL FACTOR DF ===")
    print("======================")
    print(FULL_FACTOR_DF.tail(20))

    print("")
    print("======================================")
    print("=== CREDIT CORRELATION (EMPIRICAL) ===")
    print("======================================")
    print(
        pd.DataFrame(
            np.corrcoef(CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF.values, rowvar=False),
            columns=CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF.columns,
            index=CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF.columns,
        ).round(4)
    )


def _debug_plot() -> None:
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(14, 10))

    # --- CREDIT FACTORS (returns) ---
    ax1 = fig.add_subplot(2, 1, 1)
    for col in CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF.columns:
        ax1.plot(
            CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF.index, CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF[col], label=col
        )
    ax1.set_title("Credit Factor Returns (Gaussian)")
    ax1.legend()
    ax1.axhline(0, linestyle="--", alpha=0.3)

    # --- CHI FACTORS (levels, NOT returns) ---
    ax2 = fig.add_subplot(2, 1, 2)
    for col in CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF.columns:
        ax2.plot(CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF.index, CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF[col], label=col)
    ax2.set_title("Chi-square Mixing Variables (χ²)")
    ax2.legend()

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.show()


def _debug_chi_diagnostics() -> None:
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import scipy.stats as stats

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 3, figsize=(15, 8))

    for i, col in enumerate(CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF.columns):
        data = np.asarray(CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF[col].to_numpy(), dtype=np.float64)
        nu = CANONICAL_T_COPULA_NU_BY_CHI_FACTOR[i]

        # --- Top row: histogram + PDF ---
        ax_hist = axes[0, i]
        x = np.linspace(0, np.percentile(data, 99), 200)

        ax_hist.hist(data, bins=30, density=True, alpha=0.5, label="empirical")
        ax_hist.plot(x, stats.chi2.pdf(x, df=nu), label=f"χ² pdf (ν={nu})")

        ax_hist.set_title(f"{col} Distribution")
        ax_hist.legend()

        # --- Bottom row: QQ plot ---
        ax_qq = axes[1, i]
        stats.probplot(data, dist="chi2", sparams=(nu,), plot=ax_qq)
        ax_qq.set_title(f"{col} QQ vs χ²")

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.show()


def _debug_full_correlation() -> None:
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    corr = np.corrcoef(FULL_FACTOR_DF.values, rowvar=False)

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7, 6))
    im = ax.imshow(corr, vmin=-1, vmax=1)

    ax.set_xticks(range(len(FULL_FACTOR_DF.columns)))
    ax.set_yticks(range(len(FULL_FACTOR_DF.columns)))
    ax.set_xticklabels(FULL_FACTOR_DF.columns, rotation=45, ha="right")
    ax.set_yticklabels(FULL_FACTOR_DF.columns)

    for i in range(corr.shape[0]):
        for j in range(corr.shape[1]):
            ax.text(
                j,
                i,
                f"{corr[i, j]:.2f}",
                ha="center",
                va="center",
                color="white" if abs(corr[i, j]) > 0.7 else "black",
                fontsize=7,
            )

    plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax)
    ax.set_title("Full Factor Correlation Matrix")

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.show()


def _debug_chi_moments() -> None:
    print("")
    print("========================")
    print("=== CHI MOMENT CHECK ===")
    print("========================")

    for i, col in enumerate(CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF.columns):
        data = np.asarray(CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF[col])
        nu = CANONICAL_T_COPULA_NU_BY_CHI_FACTOR[i]

        mean = data.mean()
        var = data.var()

        print(f"{col}: mean={mean:.3f} (target {nu}), var={var:.3f} (target {2 * nu})")


def _debug_chi_tail() -> None:
    print("")
    print("=======================")
    print("=== CHI TAIL RATIOS ===")
    print("=======================")

    for col in CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF.columns:
        data = np.asarray(CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF[col])

        q95 = np.percentile(data, 95)
        q99 = np.percentile(data, 99)

        print(f"{col}: q99/q95 = {q99 / q95:.3f}")


def _debug_t_factor() -> None:
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import scipy.stats as stats

    print("")
    print("=========================")
    print("=== T-FACTOR QQ CHECK ===")
    print("=========================")

    print("\nSee plot.")

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(15, 4))

    for i, (ax, col) in enumerate(
        zip(axes, CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF.columns, strict=False)
    ):
        z = CREDIT_FACTOR_RETURNS_DF[col].values
        w = CHI_MIXING_VARIABLES_DF.iloc[:, i].values
        nu = CANONICAL_T_COPULA_NU_BY_CHI_FACTOR[i]

        x = z / np.sqrt(w / nu)

        stats.probplot(x, dist="t", sparams=(nu,), plot=ax)
        ax.set_title(f"{col} → t(ν={nu})")

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.show()


def _debug_rank_correlation() -> None:
    print("")
    print("===================================")
    print("=== RANK CORRELATION (SPEARMAN) ===")
    print("===================================")

    rank_corr = 100 * FULL_FACTOR_DF.corr(method="spearman")
    print(rank_corr.round(3))


def main() -> None:
    _debug_print()
    _debug_plot()
    _debug_chi_diagnostics()
    _debug_full_correlation()
    _debug_chi_moments()
    _debug_chi_tail()
    _debug_t_factor()
    _debug_rank_correlation()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\ingest_fixtures\credit_factors\minimal_factor_universe.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/credit_factors/minimal_factor_universe.py
"""
Canonical credit-factor REGISTRY for tests and demos.

This file is structural metadata, NOT historical data. It defines:
- the set of factor *names* that obligor JTD ingest cases may reference,
- a name -> int32 UIX mapping (`factor_name_to_uix`) that
  `compile_jtd_portfolio_from_df` uses to dereference factor names into
  `CreditFactorMatrix` row indices,
- which UIX positions carry a chi-square mixing variable (t-copula), and
  the degrees of freedom for each one.

The credit code path is currently parametric-simulation only — the
copula generators draw fresh latent factors every run from
`np.random.default_rng`. No part of the credit layer consumes a
historical time series of credit-factor levels.

If/when historical or FHS scenario generation lands for credit, a
parallel `ingest_cases/credit_factors/historical/` would supply
`(T, F_credit)` factor observations through the existing
`market_data/risk_factors/matrix.py::RiskFactorMatrix` container —
F-contiguous, int32 ordinal dates, int32 factor ids — which is already
the right shape for credit history too. Until then, only the registry
metadata below is required.

Chi/nu alignment (mathematical invariant)
-----------------------------------------
Each chi-square factor in the registry has its own dof
(`nu_by_chi[c]`). Every obligor that references a chi factor MUST
have `obligor.nu == nu_by_chi[chi_uix]` — otherwise the kernel's
`sqrt(nu_i / W_i)` transform breaks the t-marginal. The canonical
t-copula ingest case is built per-obligor-chi to satisfy this:
obligor i references CHI_i with dof matching its own nu_i.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Final

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

# ==============================================================================
# FACTOR NAMES (UIX = position in this tuple)
# ==============================================================================

CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAMES: Final[tuple[str, ...]] = (
    "GLOBAL_CREDIT",  # 0  — systemic Z (Gaussian, t)
    "IDIO_1",  # 1  — idiosyncratic eps (obligor 1)
    "IDIO_2",  # 2  — idiosyncratic eps (obligor 2)
    "IDIO_3",  # 3  — idiosyncratic eps (obligor 3)
    "CHI_1",  # 4  — chi-square W_1 for obligor 1 (nu=5)
    "CHI_2",  # 5  — chi-square W_2 for obligor 2 (nu=7)
    "CHI_3",  # 6  — chi-square W_3 for obligor 3 (nu=10)
)

CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_COUNT: Final[int] = len(CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAMES)

CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX: Final[dict[str, int]] = {
    name: i for i, name in enumerate(CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAMES)
}

CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_IDS: Final[npt.NDArray[np.int32]] = np.arange(
    CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_COUNT, dtype=np.int32
)

# ==============================================================================
# T-COPULA CHI CONFIGURATION
# ==============================================================================
# One chi-square factor per obligor (uids 1, 2, 3). Each obligor's nu_i
# matches the dof of its chi factor — the only configuration that yields
# a true Student-t marginal under `sqrt(nu_i / W_i) * (beta*Z + ... )`.

CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_COLUMNS: Final[npt.NDArray[np.int32]] = np.array(
    [
        CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX["CHI_1"],
        CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX["CHI_2"],
        CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX["CHI_3"],
    ],
    dtype=np.int32,
)


CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_FACTOR_TO_NU_REGISTRY: Final[dict[str, float]] = {
    "CHI_1": 5.0,
    "CHI_2": 7.0,
    "CHI_3": 10.0,
}


# Aligned to CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_COLUMNS by index. The canonical
# t-copula ingest case uses the SAME values per-obligor.
CANONICAL_T_COPULA_NU_BY_CHI_FACTOR: Final[npt.NDArray[np.float64]] = np.array(
    list(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_FACTOR_TO_NU_REGISTRY.values()),
    dtype=np.float64,
)


def _debug_print() -> None:
    from riskquant.utils.printer import (
        print_calling_file,
        print_dict,
        print_success_message,
    )

    print_calling_file()

    print("Canonical Credit-Factor Registry - Factor Name to UIX:")
    print_dict(CANONICAL_CREDIT_FACTOR_NAME_TO_UIX)

    print("\nT-Copula Chi-Factor to Nu Registry:")
    print_dict(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_FACTOR_TO_NU_REGISTRY)
    print(f"  chi_factor_uix : {CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_COLUMNS.tolist()}")
    print(f"  nu_by_chi          : {CANONICAL_T_COPULA_NU_BY_CHI_FACTOR.tolist()}")

    print_success_message(add_space_before=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _debug_print()
```

```python
Canonical single-factor Gaussian-copula JTD ingest case.
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/jtd/copula_jtd/sf_t_minimal.py
"""
Canonical single-factor Student-t-copula JTD ingest case.

Same three obligors as the Gaussian case (uids 1-3), with per-obligor
`nu` (degrees of freedom) and a per-obligor chi factor whose dof
matches the obligor's nu. PD and LGD live on the obligor master.

Mathematical invariant: for obligor i, `nu_i` must equal the
generator's `nu_by_chi` entry for the chi factor `chi_squared_factor[i]`
points at. The canonical registry encodes this 1:1 alignment in
`CANONICAL_T_COPULA_NU_BY_CHI` — any change here must mirror there.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.credit.jtd.models import JtdModel
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.credit_factors.minimal_factor_universe import (
    CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_FACTOR_TO_NU_REGISTRY,
)
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import TCopulaJTDIngestData
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import validate_column_lengths

# ============================================================
# DATA
# ============================================================


def _get_data() -> TCopulaJTDIngestData:
    return {
        "uid": [1, 2, 3],
        "instrument": [JtdModel.COPULA_SF_T.name] * 3,
        "beta": [0.25, 0.35, 0.15],
        "systemic_factor": ["GLOBAL_CREDIT"]
        * 3,  # systemic factor is the same for all obligors
        "idiosyncratic_factor": [
            "IDIO_1",
            "IDIO_2",
            "IDIO_3",
        ],
        # Per-obligor chi factor. Each obligor's chi has its own dof
        # in the generator (CANONICAL_T_COPULA_NU_BY_CHI) and `nu`
        # below MUST match that dof. The kernel uses both: chi_uix
        # selects W_i, nu_i scales it via sqrt(nu_i / W_i).
        "chi_squared_factor": list(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_FACTOR_TO_NU_REGISTRY.keys()),
        "nu": list(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_CHI_FACTOR_TO_NU_REGISTRY.values()),
    }


CANONICAL_T_COPULA_JTD_INGEST_DATA: TCopulaJTDIngestData = _get_data()

validate_column_lengths(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_JTD_INGEST_DATA)

CANONICAL_T_COPULA_JTD_INGEST_DF = pd.DataFrame(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_JTD_INGEST_DATA)


def _debug_print() -> None:
    from riskquant.utils.printer import (
        print_calling_file,
        print_df,
        print_dict,
        print_success_message,
    )

    print_calling_file()

    print("Canonical T-Copula JTD Ingest Data:")
    print_dict(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_JTD_INGEST_DATA)

    print("As DataFrame:")
    print_df(CANONICAL_T_COPULA_JTD_INGEST_DF)

    print_success_message(add_space_before=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _debug_print()
```
